## 27 November 2026, v0.224: the new splash screen, five science drawings (task SP1)
> "Let's have the splash screen look more like the about screen with the Earth, but this time the splash screen is the orbit that I selected with S2. And I also like the harmonograph as well, S5. So let's have multiple that randomly show. I like S1 as well, as a random one. And let's remove the logo in the center. And the AudioLab Pro logo needs to match the colors of the animation in the background. The loading bar is a real loading bar. The spectrogram is also a cool one, again without logo [...] And for all of them, the loading bar's colors should also match. And no text. And let's give spectrogram a lower chance of popping up. And the orbit chart is also cool. [...] all the lines and the grid around it much more subtle. I'm mostly talking about the text around the edges. [...] H3, H1, H2, let's keep that. And move Digital Audio Workstation, the version and the loading bar a little bit more down to make room for the circle."
> On timing (final): "The splash should stay there as long as it's loading, and then it should stay there another second or two. And when it's there for the last 2 seconds, you can also press it to close it."

Started from the drawing code of the pick page of task D6 (`docs/science-look.md`, options S1, S2, S5, S6, S7). No other task had work in progress on `index.html`.

### Built
- **Five pictures, one per start, drawn in code on the one canvas of the splash** (`SPLBG`, a script right after the splash at the top of `<body>`, about 11 kB; it has to sit there, before the app's code, so the picture is on screen at the first paint):
  - **Moons in resonance (S2)**: Io, Europa and Ganymede at 4, 2 and 1 orbits in the same time. New against the pick page: Jupiter stands in the centre where the logo was, as a fine circle with three faint bands, and the orbits and the planet are to scale (5.9, 9.4 and 15 Jupiter radii). The names ride on the moons.
  - **Harmonograph (S5)**: a pen on two slowing pendulums; one of 3:2, 4:3, 5:4 or 2:1 on each start, the next one after 13 s if the splash stays up. It starts with a third of the figure drawn and finishes in 4.5 s, because the splash is short.
  - **Dipole field (S1)**: field lines r = L sin²θ, tilted 10 degrees, with a small planet in line work in the centre; the lines begin at its surface. The charges now bounce between their two mirror points along their line (as trapped particles do), where the pick page let them run through. The lines fade out behind the name.
  - **Orbit chart (S7)**: H1, H2 and H3 circling at 1, 2 and 3 times the same rate around a small nucleus, tick ring, grid.
  - **Spectrogram (S6)**: the made-up 145 BPM pattern, rolling, in the upper 64 % of the card, fading into the card above the name.
- **Chance**: the four line drawings equal, the spectrogram a third of each of them (`SPEC_CHANCE = 1/3`, one constant in `SPLBG`). Never the same one twice in a row (the last one is kept in `localStorage` `audiolab-splash-last`). Because of that rule the long-run shares are 22.7 % for each of the four and 9.1 % for the spectrogram.
- **No logo, no text at the edges.** The atom is no longer drawn on the loading splash (the About window keeps it). Gone from the pick page versions: the captions in the corners, the date stamp, the Hz scale, "V 0.219 · READY". Kept: H1, H2, H3 and the moon names with their 4, 2, 1, which ride on the bodies. The line under the bar that named the loading step ("Starting the audio engine" ...) is no longer shown (it is still there for screen readers).
- **More subtle lines** than on the pick page: grid 0.022 and 0.05 (was 0.045 and 0.1), cross-hair 0.10 (0.28) and it stops above the name, tick ring 0.17 (0.4), orbit rings 0.20 to 0.24 (0.30 to 0.38), field lines 0.22 (0.3). All lines 1 px.
- **Colours from the picture**: each picture has its own set (`SPLBG.PAL`): "Audio", the gradient of "Lab", the PRO pill, the two small lines, the bar and the bright end of the bar. Moons cyan to blue with an amber end; harmonograph periwinkle to lilac; dipole blue with an amber end; orbit chart amber; spectrogram cyan to yellow (its own colour scale). The two small lines are lighter than before (contrast about 6 : 1; the version line was about 3 : 1).
- **Layout**: the loading splash is still a card over the app (the app builds up behind it as before), now larger and without glass (opaque, no backdrop blur): 46 % of the window width, between 560 and 1280 px, height 0.74 of the width, never more than 94 % of the window. The figure has the upper two thirds; the name, "Digital audio workstation", the version and the bar sit at the bottom; under them the hint line. All sizes follow the card. The card lies above the "Demo song loaded" message, which used to sit lower than the old small card.
- **Timing** (block "v0.224 (task SP1)" before `askChoice`, names `SP1`, `sp1Ready`, `sp1Close`): loading is unchanged (`bootRun` of v0.195: interface, audio engine, fonts, first picture, the page settled; the bar follows them). When it is done the app behind is unlocked at once (`bootClean`), the splash stays `SP1.hold` = 1500 ms more, then fades in 0.26 s. In that stretch "Click to continue" shows at the bottom; a click anywhere or any key closes it at once. A click on the card and a plain key are not passed on to the app (Space does not start the song); a click beside the card does what it does and also closes the splash; shortcuts with Ctrl or Cmd pass. **Before loading is done nothing closes it**: Esc used to close the loading splash at any time, it no longer does.
- **Choice made:** the 1.5 s apply to the start of the app. The same splash is also shown for a moment when a project is opened inside the running app (`withSplash`); there it leaves right after loading, as before (`SP1.holdOpen` = 0), because 1.5 s on every project switch would only slow the work. One number if it should hold there too.
- **Show now** in Settings shows a random picture on the same card ("Click to close").
- Edit script `tools/edits/taskSP1.py` with its three parts (`taskSP1_early.js`, `taskSP1_css.txt`, `taskSP1_block.js`), every replacement asserted unique. One line of the old `showSplash` changed (its atom loop stops for the new card, and for a splash that was replaced).

### Cost
- One canvas, no blur, no shadows, no gradient fills per frame for the line drawings (the card's background is CSS). At most 60 pictures a second (every second frame on a 120 Hz screen), 30 in the lighter looks. Canvas pixels capped at 2 per point and 2.2 million in all. The spectrogram is a picture 220 points wide that the screen scales up; 30 new columns a second, nothing drawn in between.
- While the code is being read the page cannot draw: the first picture stands still and only the bar moves (on the compositor, as since v0.195). The animation starts when the page can draw again.
- Stopped the moment the splash closes; the canvas goes with the card 0.3 s later. Frames are asked for through the app's frame gate, so the background freeze (task AO) holds them. "Reduce motion": one finished still picture, no frames.
- Drawing time per picture at 1600 x 1000 on the cloud machine (no graphics card): moons 0.04 to 0.16 ms, harmonograph 0.06 to 0.15, dipole 0.10 to 0.19, orbit chart 0.05 to 0.07, spectrogram 0.01 to 0.02. The atom it replaces took 3.7 to 4.3 ms a picture on the same machine (v0.218 entry).
- **Load time, before and after** (`tools/ui-tests/loadtime.py`, now also prints `rdy`, the moment loading is done; headless, normal starts). In one browser, alternating, 16 starts each: loading done at 1536 ms on v0.223 (1408 to 2060), and at 1478, 1515 and 1595 ms on v0.224 with the orbit chart, the harmonograph and the spectrogram forced (1370 to 2142). First paint 576 against 578 to 584 ms; interface built 1072 against 1066 to 1079; from interface to settled 414 against 416 to 419. **No difference outside the noise.** The splash then stays 1500 ms more (measured 1500 to 1552).

### Tested (`tools/ui-tests/taskSP1.py`, headless Chromium, 149 checks, all pass)
- Each of the five forced through `localStorage` `audiolab-splash-test` (`moons`, `harm`, `dipole`, `chart`, `spec`) at 1280 x 720, 1920 x 1080 and 3840 x 2160: the forced picture is the one shown, one canvas, the card inside the window, name, two lines, bar and hint in order and apart, the text block in the lower third, and the strongest pixel of the picture under each piece of text at most 40 of 255 (the faint grid). The only texts are the name, the two lines and the hint. The colours on the name and the bar are the picture's. Screenshots of all fifteen looked at.
- Timing, three normal starts and one with the CPU slowed 4 times: the splash is never ready before the last loading step; it closes by itself 1500 to 1550 ms after (1540 to 2160 ms with the slowed CPU, where timers run late); afterwards no animation, no splash, no canvas, the app unlocked.
- While loading: Esc, Enter, a letter, Space and a click leave it up, and there is no hint line. In the last stretch: a click, Space, Esc and a letter each close it at once; Space is not passed on; right after, Space plays.
- Pick: 60,000 picks, never the same twice in a row; shares 22.8, 22.7, 22.6, 22.8 and 9.2 % (expected 22.7 and 9.1). 14 real starts in a row: never the same twice, all five seen.
- Frame rate in the Show now preview: 45 to 60 pictures a second here, 25 to 30 in the lighter looks, never above 60 and 30. Frame gate shut: the picture stops; open: it runs again. Reduce motion: one still picture.
- Splash switched off in Settings: none, no picture running. About: unchanged, with its atom. Splash inside the running app: up for 0.65 s, no hold.
- No console errors in any of it.
- **Fractals levels unchanged** (`tools/engine-tests/export_same.py`): Kick -19.05560 dB, Bass -35.48685 dB with the master at -10 dB; -9.75669 and -25.48699 dB at 0 dB; the other checksums as before.
- Regression: `taskAO.py` (quick, all pass), `taskU.py`, `taskAI.py`, `play.py`, `fxlevels.py`, `overview.py` pass; `glass_gs1.py` 64 of 65 (the one is its own check for version 0.223).

### Not verified
- The user's Mac: real graphics card, retina, 120 and 240 Hz, how the first still picture reads while the code loads there.
- The desktop app (Electron) was not started; the page is the same file and no desktop shell file changed.
- A window zoomed with the app's own zoom setting: the card is sized for it in the code, not looked at.

### Left
- Keys pressed while the splash is still loading go to the app as before (Space starts the song behind the card). Not changed.
- The spectrogram is the made-up pattern; the last open project's own sound, as the pick page suggested, is a later step.
- The harmonograph's figure differs a lot between the four intervals; some sit off-centre in the card.

### Release
- v0.224: commit f2d766b on main (`index.html` was not touched by other sessions during the run).
- v0.224. App copy synced, update note written ("New splash screen: five science drawings that change from start to start (moons in resonance, harmonograph, magnetic field, orbit chart, spectrogram), with the name and the loading bar in the drawing's colours. It stays about 1.5 seconds after loading; a click or a key closes it sooner."). MIN_SHELL not raised, no desktop shell file changed, no desktop build. ## 27 November 2026: task NB-N1 (native audio output and device control, as a helper)

No app changes: `index.html` and `desktop/` were not touched, no version. Running list: `docs/native-mac-build.md`. Test app 0.2: https://github.com/Audio-lab-DAW/Audio-Lab-Pro-Alpha/releases/tag/native-lab-2 .

Started in the NM1 session after the user's tests on their Mac and their words "yes continue ... do it without my input". Logged under task NB's name so that NB goes on from N2.

### Built
- **`alengine`** (`native/engine/`): owns the audio output, driven by JSON lines: devices and formats, open with rate, buffer, format and exclusive use, move to another device while playing, volume and mute, status; changes from outside come as events (volume, rate, buffer, exclusive, devices, default output). Sound comes in through a local socket and goes to the device untouched; a ring corrects itself when it runs empty or too full and counts it. Integer formats that the system does not mix are written by the engine itself (16, 24, 32 bit).
- **`native/bridge/engine-bridge.js`**: the desktop app's side as a plain Node module.
- **Test app 0.2**: Rate menu, "Exclusive use of the device", "Device volume" both ways, a device list that follows plugging, a clear line when the test pattern has nothing to play.
- Workflow `native-lab.yml`: engine checks, the hand-over test through the loop-back device, the Node bridge test, a picture of the app's main window.

### Tested (build machine, virtual M1, run 45939789700)
- Hand-over: 287,998 of 287,998 samples identical at the device, fed by a test program and fed by Node through the bridge. One earlier run had one gap of 1,456 samples in the Node test, together with one overload reported by the machine.
- Open 48 to 73 ms, switch while playing 64 to 124 ms, rate 48 to 96 kHz in 124 ms and noticed by the engine, buffers 256, 64 and 32 accepted, volume set and read back, a change by another program reported at once, exclusive use keeps a second program out (error 35).
- Arithmetic of the integer formats and of the ring: all checks pass on macOS and Linux.
- Test app 0.2 built, started on the build machine, loaded Surge XT and played as before; the new row of controls is in place in the picture.

### Not verified
- No hand has clicked the new controls. No real DAC: the integer path never ran with sound, hot-plug was not seen, bit-perfect to hardware cannot be shown here.
- Drift between two clocks over minutes (the test is 3 seconds); the smooth correction is not built.
- Nothing is inside the app yet.

### Assumptions
JSON lines and a local socket instead of shared memory; "exact with rare corrections" before "always smooth"; exclusive use is released on a device change.

### Next
N2 (the engine under the app) needs `index.html` and `desktop/`; left for task NB in its turn, after the design chain, as the user ordered. For the user: try 0.2's second row with the Gryphon and paste the report.

## 27 November 2026: task NB-N2a (the road from the desktop shell to the native engine, proven outside the app)

No app changes: `index.html` and `desktop/` were not touched. Details and the table: `docs/native-mac-build.md`.

- **Built:** `tools/native-lab/electron-tap/` (a test program on the real shell, Electron 45.4.1): an AudioWorklet hands each 128-frame block through a message port from the audio thread straight to the shell's main process, which feeds `alengine` through `native/bridge/engine-bridge.js`. `tools/native-lab/stream_compare.py` compares what was sent with what the loop-back device recorded, bit for bit, and finds its place again after gaps.
- **Tested (build machine, run 45942655178, 20 s):** all 7,500 blocks arrived in both runs, nothing lost. Page clock from no device (`sinkId: none`): the page ran at 0.59 of real time, the ring ran empty 79 times, and every one of 1,920,000 samples that reached the device was identical. Page clock from a real device playing silence: 0.998 of real time, 1,917,384 of 1,920,000 identical, 5 gaps with 4 overloads reported by the machine.
- **Decided from it (changeable):** in N2 the page keeps a real device as its clock and plays silence into it; with exclusive use on the DAC that clock device must be another one. Blocks are sent as copies (transferred buffers arrive empty in the main process). N2 needs the smooth drift correction.
- **Not verified:** nothing inside AudioLab, no real DAC, nothing longer than 20 seconds, nothing on the M4.
- **Stopped here on purpose:** the next step needs a hook in `index.html` and changes in `desktop/`. Only one task may edit `index.html` at a time, the design chain is working there, and the user's order is design first, then the native engine. Task NB continues from N2 when it is fired in its turn.

## 27 November 2026, v0.225: the new About window, Earth with its radiation belts and the fine atom (task AB1)
> "I like E2. I like E3. Actually, E7 is better than E3. So let's have E7 and E2 randomized. One or the other opens. And let's make the animated logo on the top a little larger and more anatomically correct with more particles, more fidelity and resolution. Wanted to really fit in to the whole splash screen based on design rules. I want it to be really fine and subtle with small little particles and dots with high fidelity that would work on high resolution screens. I want it to look cool and subtle."
> Picks bar: "About round 2: P2 S3 B1 C3 L2"

Started from the drawing code of the pick page of task SR7 (`docs/about-round-2.md`, read from the artifact). No other task had work in progress on `index.html` (the `NB-N1` wip commits touch only the native lab).

### How the picks were read
- Two pictures, **one at random each time About opens** (an even chance, no restart needed, the same one can come twice): **corner** = E2 "Corner chart" (large Earth low right, cut by the edge, soft glowing belts, 9 shells, 93 dots) and **sheet** = E7 "Chart sheet" (Earth on the left edge, 15 shells of thin contour lines, 100 dots). Each keeps its own placement and belt style.
- Shared by both, from the mix line: the Earth drawn as **S3** (drafted: heavier outline, graticule every 15 degrees, coastlines, night side shaded, axis ticks, turning), the content as **C3** (spec list), the logo as **L2** (the animated atom stays). E7 was S3 and C3 already; E2 becomes exactly the mix line.

### Built
- **The window** (`AB1`, block "v0.225 (task AB1)" before `askChoice`; CSS before `.dacv`, class `ab2` on `#splash.ab`). `showSplash(true)` now opens it (`showSplash` and `hideSplash` are wrapped once more; the loading splash, its preview and the old atom code are untouched, and the old `aboutHtml` is no longer called). Same window size as before (780 wide, 92 % of the height up to 860).
- **The picture**: one canvas behind everything; it stays in place while the list scrolls. No text in it. Field lines are dipole lines r = L cos²(latitude) around an axis leaning 9.2 degrees toward 80.8 N, 72.8 W; every dot sits on a drawn line and bounces between its two mirror points with the real speed profile (tables as in round 2); outer-belt lines with their electrons drift eastward, inner-belt lines with their protons westward; the Earth turns once in 4 minutes. New against the pick page: the Earth follows the real window (corner: 66 and 74 points from the right and bottom edge; sheet: on the left edge at 42.5 % of the height), lines beyond L 3 are drawn with 80 points instead of 40 (the large arcs showed corners), and the dark veil behind the text is measured from the page (the text column, the name, a soft round patch behind the atom) instead of fixed numbers. Dots inside the veil are drawn at about a fifth of their strength, as in round 2.
- **Colours from the picture** (round 2 table): ground #05080e to #0b1322, rules and outlines in the field-line blue #7aaaff, values and "Under the hood" in the outer belt's cyan #6ed0ee, text in blue-tinted greys #e8eef9, #a3b3cd, #8fa0bb, the "Lab" gradient cyan to blue. The warm tone of the inner belt (#f0a878) is used by the atom only. The amber heading, the purple in the name, the coloured icon tiles and the hint line at the bottom are gone.
- **Content, C3**: logo, name, "Digital audio workstation · Version", designed by Ilja V. Jakovliev, the eight values (version, build, audio engine, running at, instruments, effects, platform, cores) and the six highlighted features as a spec list with a hairline at the left of each entry, then "All 35 features" and "Check for updates", then the copyright line. Same facts as before, all from `AHF`; the six short value lines are the pick page's wording (`AB1` `SHORT`). "All 35 features" opens the full list in the same quiet style (six groups, name, measured value, one sentence; no icons, no cards) and the veil then covers the whole column; "Show less" closes it. Hovering a feature shows its sentence, as before.
- **The fine atom** (`FATOM`, one small piece: `FATOM.paint(g,cx,cy,size,t,o)` draws into any canvas context, `FATOM.canvas(cv,size,t,o)` sizes a canvas for the screen and draws). The AudioLab mark as particles: three orbits at 30, 90 and 150 degrees, each a circle seen so that it shows as an ellipse 0.42 wide (the proportions of `mark-colour.svg`), a small nucleus, one warm electron as in the mark. 384 fine dots on the three orbits (each on its own circle, slightly spread in radius and tilt), 80 fainter ones on other circles further in, a nucleus of 52 dots (half warm, half pale), three electrons with a short tail: 519 in all. Every particle runs on a proper circle around the nucleus at the speed Kepler's rule gives for its radius (one turn in 11 s on the main orbits, faster closer in); particles behind the nucleus are hidden, the far half of each orbit is dimmer and smaller, the near half brighter; the whole atom sways a few degrees so the depth shows. Hairline orbits (0.6 points), dots 0.4 to 0.7 points. 116 points across (the pick page's atom was 72). Drawn at the device pixel ratio (and the app's zoom) up to 3, at sub-pixel positions, no scaling of a ready picture, no blur. Nothing outside About uses it yet (the nav logo and the old atom code are unchanged); it is meant for the orbit loader of task MD1 later.
- **Test hook**: `AB1.force('corner')`, `AB1.force('sheet')`, `AB1.force(null)`, or `localStorage` `audiolab-about-test`. Counters `AB1.n` (pictures drawn, atoms drawn, openings per picture).
- Edit script `tools/edits/taskAB1.py` with `taskAB1_block.js` and `taskAB1_css.txt`, every replacement asserted unique.
- A bug found on the way: the window first carried `data-ab`, which the mixer's A/B switch listens for, so "All 35 features" switched the mix compare. It is `data-abp` now; the test clicks through the list with a song loaded.

### Cost
- One canvas for the picture, one small one for the atom. The picture at most 30 times a second, the atom at most 60, both from one frame request. Picture canvas at most 2 pixels per point. No blur, no shadows. Nothing is worked out before About is opened for the first time (tables, coastlines and particles are built then).
- Runs only while About is open: closing stops it at once. Frames are asked for through the app's frame gate, so the background freeze (task AO) holds them. Light level and "reduce motion": one still picture of each, no frames; switching the Light level while About is open is followed.
- Drawing time on the cloud machine (no graphics card), off screen at 2 pixels per point: corner 1.0 to 1.4 ms at 780 x 720 and 0.6 ms at 780 x 860, sheet 0.4 to 0.5 ms, the atom 0.25 ms. At 30 and 60 a second that is about 2 to 4 % of one core for the picture plus 1.5 % for the atom, only while About is open. The old About drew its atom in 3.7 to 4.3 ms on every frame (v0.218 entry).
- **Frame time while the demo plays with About open** (1500 x 950, headless, 6 s each, two runs each): v0.224 20.2 and 19.5 ms on average; v0.225 corner 19.7 and 19.9 ms, sheet 19.2 and 19.1 ms. With About closed 16.9 to 17.1 ms in all. **Not worse than before**; the longest frame 67 ms against 100 ms.

### Tested (`tools/ui-tests/taskAB1.py`, headless Chromium, 192 checks, all pass)
- Both pictures forced at 1280 x 720, 1920 x 1080 and 1920 x 1080 at device pixel ratio 2, moving and at the Light level (24 windows): the forced picture is shown, two canvases, the window inside the screen, nothing to scroll, the picture canvas at the pixel ratio, the atom canvas 116 points and 116 or 232 pixels, the parts in order and apart, 8 values and 6 features, no old About markup, no em dash. Screenshots of all looked at, the atom zoomed in at pixel ratio 2.
- **Contrast**, text colour against the brightest single point of the picture under each piece of text, six moments moving and the still Light picture, worst case over all windows (moving / Light): "Audio" 16.0 / 17.0, "Lab" 7.7 / 8.2, the small line 6.6 / 6.8, Designed by 8.4 / 8.2, the name 15.3 / 15.8, info labels 6.5 / 6.8, info values 15.0 / 15.1, "Under the hood" 10.0 / 10.5, feature names 15.1 / 15.1, feature values 9.3 / 9.4, copyright 6.6 / 6.9, buttons 14.0 / 14.1. Everything above 6 : 1 (the first build had 4.6 : 1 where the Earth's outline passed the edge of the column; the veil now covers the column to its edge).
- **Frames counted**: closed, nothing is drawn (after every closing, and after 40 openings in a row); open and moving, 28 to 32 pictures and at most 63 atoms a second; Light level and reduce motion, no frames after the still picture; frame gate shut, nothing; open again, it runs.
- **Random choice**: 20,000 picks, 49.3 to 50.0 % corner, runs of the same one up to 13 long; 40 real openings in a row gave both, without a restart.
- The list: 35 features in 6 groups, scrolls, no sideways scroll, the picture stays put while it scrolls, closes again. "Check for updates" opens the update window. Esc and the close button close. Settings, General, the About button opens the new window. The nav logo is still the app's own atom; the splash preview is not the About window.
- The app's zoom at 125 % and 80 %: canvases at the screen's real pixels (looked at 125 %). A 700 x 560 window: inside the screen, scrolls down, no sideways scroll.
- No console errors in any of it.
- **Fractals levels unchanged** (`tools/engine-tests/export_same.py`): Kick -19.05560 dB, Bass -35.48685 dB with the master at -10 dB; -9.75669 and -25.48699 dB at 0 dB; Saw, Pad, Acid, Wavetable and Venom the same checksums as v0.224. (Plant, the two Sampler lines and Drum kit differ between two runs of the same file, also on v0.224; they use chance.) No audio code was touched.
- Regression: `taskSP1.py` (149 of 149), `taskAO.py` quick (all pass when run alone; one timing check, "the first frame starts after 23 ms", missed once while another test ran beside it), `about_showcase.py` (its selectors moved to the new window), `ifd.py`, `play.py`, `overview.py`, `fxlevels.py`, `taskU.py`, `taskAI.py` pass.

### Not verified
- The user's Mac: a real graphics card, retina, 120 Hz, how fine the atom reads there. The cloud machine draws in software.
- The desktop app (Electron) was not started; the page is the same file and no desktop shell file changed.
- The light themes of the app: About is a dark window in every theme, as before; "Light" here is the Light performance level.

### Left

- In a window lower than about 640 points the content is taller than the window and scrolls (as the old About did).
- A click on the dimmed app beside the window goes to the app, as before.
- The atom elsewhere (nav logo, orbit loader of task MD1, splash) is not started.
